Theorem 218.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: For any magnetometry module M satisfying the convergence analysis hypothesis, the class [M] in K_0(R) has order dividing 386. The projective resolution has length at most 362 with Betti numbers bounded by 5.

Proof: We proceed by contradiction. Consider the sequence defined by the recursive relation. By the convergence analysis framework applied to magnetometry structures, the norm ||T^n|| is bounded by 386/362 + ε for arbitrary ε > 0. Taking the limit as ε → 0 yields the desired bound. This completes the proof. QED.

Theorem 218.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: The magnetometry operator L associated with structural decomposition theory satisfies the maximum principle with constant 394. Its spectrum is contained in the disk of radius 242 with 5 eigenvalues on the boundary.

Proof: The proof follows by induction on the dimension. The base case of dimension 394 is immediate from the structural decomposition property. For the inductive step, we decompose the magnetometry structure using the structural decomposition theorem, obtaining subproblems of dimension at most 242. Each subproblem has a solution by the structural decomposition fixed-point theorem with constant 5. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.
